# Independent source-fidelity and correction audit

This is the one authoritative second-pass record for the existing eight provisional syntheses. It corrects and selectively strengthens them at the same ongoing V15/FB02 boundary, preserving their depth, frozen observations and version history. The distinct [Japanese speech/interlocutor audit](MEDALIST_JAPANESE_SPEECH_AND_INTERLOCUTOR_AUDIT_THROUGH_V15.md) owns located printed-language examples and routes supported conclusions into the existing studies. Neither audit is primary literary evidence.

## Method, coverage and count rules

**Owner's guiding principle: analytical accuracy and source fidelity take precedence over process.** Integrity procedures serve that goal. A known source-verified error must not remain current analytical authority merely because a routine process preserves its earlier wording. When a procedure obstructs a necessary correction, identify the exact conflict, use a narrow documented exception within the owner's authorized scope, preserve before/after evidence and provenance, and verify the resulting downstream meaning. Here current claims and syntheses are corrected while earlier formulations remain explicitly historical with operative errata. No procedural green result substitutes for original-source inspection. This principle does not supply missing evidence or expand the owner's separately stated source, path and no-main boundaries; unresolved operational status is reported truthfully.

This is a **staged independent reassessment, not a blind study**. Existing syntheses, frozen readings and owner flags informed target selection. Readers inspected adequate original Japanese images and saved source accounts before their final comparisons; later targeted returns for numerical/protocol details are disclosed. Root reviewed the semantic records and corrected prose, and directly inspected the mandatory rival scene and other consequential late pages plus diagnostic cross-checks. A hash or identifier check is supplementary to that human-readable semantic work.

All32 required corpus texts were read completely across the team:15 frozen manga readings,2 frozen fanbook audits,7 cumulative ledgers and8 syntheses. Current checklist/AGENTS/authority, entrypoint, method, architecture, reading/reconstruction specifications, source locks, readiness/role-gap/release audits and applicable publication policies were recovered. Complete text reading does not mean a new complete original-media reading. The receipt matrix distinguishes fully read text from selective additional reads and perception from byte processing.

There are **60 distinct consequential principal clusters**, not60 citation IDs. Two overlapping proposals were consolidated:EC11 into LC05’s childhood/cost frame and JC12 into FC04’s duration test. JC06 separately tests the attested perceptual faculty and its unproved origin. Baseline formulations/current claim versions below refer to f81; the accepted output advances MD-CL-0012@15→@16 (MD-RV-0279) and MD-CL-0023@9→@10 (MD-RV-0280). The latter closes a remaining light-motif propagation defect found after the first author commit. Other current formulations survive with explicit historical errata or bounded derivative repairs.

## Mandatory rival-attribution determination

Original V13/Score52/W01 EPUB0100 gives Hikaru’s unballooned thought:「あなたのライバルにふさわしいのは私だけ」, intensified by「最強の私だけ」. The nearby nameいのりちゃん fixesあなた as Inori;私 is Hikaru. The suitability direction is **strongest Hikaru→suitable rival for Inori**. It does not say Inori alone is suitable for Hikaru.

Occurrences0101–0102 show a reaching hand and Inori seated behind the judges, with separate Inori thought oriented toward光ちゃん as hope/light. Neither formulation has depicted speaking/hearing. Inori demonstrably receives Hikaru’s earlier thicket promise to skate for her and sees the performance/gesture; she is not shown hearing the exclusivity proposition. Hikaru is not shown hearing Inori’s light thought or knowing the hidden coach-debt belief. Calling the composition an answer may describe reader-visible juxtaposition, never a literal information transfer.

The integrated sentence reversed direction and transformed interiority into a communicated correction. The supporting study had the direction closer but still claimed receipt. Both current passages are repaired. MD-RL-0300, the V13 MD-PAIR-0005 projection, MD-CS-0279 and MD-VF-0116 receive retained-history errata; MD-RL-0301 remains Inori→Hikaru. MD-KN-0127 already correctly separates the relevant knowledge and is preserved. The full dependency search covered all eight syntheses and seven ledgers. MD-CL-0003@14 remains a bounded self-presentation/autonomy claim; it does not require the false utterance. Reciprocal recognition survives through actual peer dialogue, performance and private orientation with their different channels.

### Light-motif propagation closure after the first author commit

The first author commit, `1c27508547c52fd04159ea0bcbf7ee5d01c761fb`, repaired the mandatory suitability direction but left the form motif table (“the name received as hoped”), its key-word paragraph (“used by each girl of the other in V13”) and active `MD-CL-0023@9` insufficiently bounded. The parent's independent follow-up exposed that residual defect. This is disclosed rather than counted as a new principal cluster.

Root then inspected original V12/W01 CBZ0047–0049 at1125×1600 and rechecked the previously viewed V13/W01 EPUB0101–0102. V12 dates the train departure to4September, maps the later November encounter and gives Hikaru's solitary resolve「希望の光に私はなるんだ」. The first-person subject is Hikaru and the intended light is herself. In V13, Inori privately regards Hikaru as the light that keeps illuminating her. The formulation is not Hikaru calling Inori that light, and no hearing of either private statement is depicted. The frozen V13 Written Japanese section already distinguishes heroine→rival from rival→herself; its rhetorical “answer” remains frozen and is governed by the channel errata.

The correction advances `MD-CL-0023@9→@10` / `MD-RV-0280`, repairs both operative form passages and the form/Hikaru dependency sections, clarifies Inori's private naming, and appends visual/character/relationship history errata. The three affected syntheses advance to1.2; the other five remain1.1. The follow-up changes no freeze, source boundary, stable observation, prediction, readiness outcome or operational rule.

The three V12 diagnostic pages were absent from the first489-page union and therefore increase the current distinct admitted inspection count to492 (V12:26→29; root:69→72; observer-page total521→524, with32 duplicates still removed). The existing27 formal visual sequences/199 occurrences and60 principal verdicts remain unchanged; these three pages are channel/wording checks, not a newly claimed formal sequence. The prior private489-page receipt is preserved; the current supplemental receipt records exact members, hashes, dimensions and actual perception, reflected in the sanitized manifest below. Frozen reading bodies and all pre-follow-up ledger rows are retained.

## Adversarial integrated-interpretation tests

These are qualitative source rereadings and counterreading tests, not held-out behavioral tests. V15 explicitness is weighed against earlier direct evidence rather than treated as origin of every motive.

| Alternative | Early direct evidence | Middle direct evidence | Late direct evidence | Verdict / what would discriminate |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
|Athletic achievement and recognition governs|V01 skill trial/choice;V02 wants wins/losses/medals;V03 loss and protocol|V06 gold exchange;V07 first quad;V09 warm-up and novice comparison;V11 standings|V13 cut/changed rival program;V14 calls/PCS;V15 short-program margins|Survives as an independent structure; athletic achievement cannot alone explain support secrecy and concern about others’ work. It must not be reduced to debt. |
|Personal autonomy/right to choose governs|V01 choice influenced by admiration;V03 rest and V04 veto coexist with reported joint decision|V07 explained childchoice versus explicit delegation;V09 private layout move;V10 peercreed|V13 Hikaru’s own skate and stated release;V15 choice to carry plus movie self-erasure|Survives with welfare/knowledge limits; chosen burden can be agency or costly constraint. Future disagreement/conduct would test durable autonomy. |
|Reciprocal recognition governs|V01 affirmation before skill;V04 secret wish to keep choosing coach|V07 sleepy born-to-skate recognition,unreceived vow;V09 accusation/thanks;V10 peerpromise|V13 actual thicket promise/private rivalry;V15 pupil/coach commitments and mother’s refusal|Survives after the rival error:recognized relation does not require communicated exclusivity or complete mutual knowledge. Receipt/use/effect must remain separate. |
|Ambition, delight, artistry and burden coexist|V01 dancing/beauty and win together;V02 pleasure/cold concern|V05 mastery, V07 sensory cue, V09 hunger/pleasure and rain disagreement|V14 personal beauty/competition criteria,film program and clean skate;V15 comic food delight,chosen burden and unresolved promise|Strongest constraint on reduction. Permission→cost organizes a persistent problem but is not an exhaustive structure or a universal causal diagnosis. |

The permission→cost/debt thesis **survives with bounds**. Private cost worry already appears in V01 choice and V04 coach protection; V15 makes the belief unusually explicit rather than creating it. Actual athletic/creative ends remain real. Childhood cost/debt helps explain Tsukasa’s help-refusal/self-sacrifice but does not uniquely create the attested eye or all coaching strengths. Support-as-participation is voiced by several differently situated people, not an adult consensus or an established winning method. Kago, Kanayumi and Daria are distinct independent lines of argument; Tsukasa’s later application is not a fourth independent source of the same idea, preserving the existing release qualification.

Hikaru’s attempted own skating is shown; post-V13 autonomous life is unshown. Yodaka’s departure and stated reason are supported, while hidden motives and future return remain open. V14’s successful response to a remembered fall is not recovery after an actual new error. V13 bench restatement and V15 stairwell restatement establish reception without proving durable debt release. Inori’s role account concerns present cost; the older career-ending belief is neither explicitly confessed nor demonstrably withdrawn. The source allows meaningful disagreement about care, shielding, ambition and burden.

Naming/referent tests locate Gori’s doctrine, Kanayumi’s life-sacrifice diagnosis, Riley’s pain/rehearsal conjectures, child injury referents and imagined mentor scenes with their actual owners. No older rejected deprivation hypothesis becomes Inori’s proven childhood cause. Public statements, remembered speech, creator commentary, private thoughts and received dialogue remain different source layers. No current Hikaru/Yodaka state is filled after their last continuity appearance; no final outcome is forecast.

## Reconstruction readiness and monograph decision

MD-RD-0080–0082 keep three levels separate:substantial bounded descriptive psychology; located conditional interlocutor patterns/tendencies with counterexamples; and adequately tested behavioral rules, for which evidence remains insufficient. The selected language contexts are not a frequency census, a held-out set or response-transfer test. No MD-RULE is promoted and no operational character record is created.

Inori has strong located ambition/choice, face-reading/knowledge, family/peer/coach language and role evidence, with counterexamples to simple compliance or autonomy. Tsukasa has strong attested skill, listening/change, service/neglect and register evidence, with unproved faculty origin and untested promise. Hikaru has enough evidence for a future narrowly scoped V01–V13 monograph covering her own motives, self-presentation, interlocutors and counterexamples, but the present joint supporting study already holds that responsibility. No new full monograph is necessary to repair this checkpoint; symmetry is not a reason to manufacture it. If later separately authorized, its canonical home should remain the existing hikaru-kamisaki directory, with Yodaka/formation comparison retained in the joint study. Ordinary post-departure life, guardianship and durable autonomy remain unshown.

The full diff must pass the live CHANGE_INTEGRATION_CHECKLIST, current/index routing preflight for series/medalist, UTF8/whitespace, artifact identity/ownership, locator/member/hash/dimension integrity, frozen-history equality, claim-version/ID and actual downstream semantic checks. Built-in repository validation does not prove Japanese semantics or MD-ID proposition correctness; the source accounts, counterreadings and revised-prose readback above supply that work. No raw scan, EPUB/CBZ/OCR dump, alternate permanent ledger, architecture replacement or unauthorized model belongs in this change.

The published source commit is recovered from the containing Git revision, rather than embedding its future hash in its own contents. Fresh source/main refs, author identity, exact explicit staged paths and ordinary branch ancestry must be checked immediately before commit/push. Only series/medalist is pushed, without force. Publication is complete only when exact-source full audit, required housekeeping and final exact-head Repository integration audit are confirmed; older301-test/main77bd receipts do not validate this new tranche. Exact run/head receipts and ending SHA belong in draftPR167 and the final handoff. The draft hold remains after validation.

Root staged an entrypoint-only CLOSED trial; native current/index routing preflight failed with “series/medalist/CURRENT_STATE_AND_CORPUS_MAP.md: substantive sequential analysis requires sequential_analysis_lock OPEN”. Validator lines1925–1939 require OPEN for the fifteen classified manga readings introduced after the immutable activation baseline unless a reviewed terminal_closures record preserves them behind CLOSED. Root did not alter the baseline, validator or global control. After this diagnosis the owner explicitly answered “Keep series-only scope; report the lock blocker”. The published entrypoint therefore retains OPEN with this explicit inconsistency, completed/no-next-source state and CLOSED supplemental lane; it does not relabel OPEN as permission for reassessment or new source admission. A separately authorized, reviewed narrow global closure record plus matching terminal front-matter states is needed to close the source-admission gate. This specific follow-up remains blocked by scope, while the authorized source-fidelity corrections can pass existing publication validation.

## Remaining priorities, ranked by analytical consequence

1. **Durable receipt/use and conduct:**only later authorized evidence could test the stairwell promise, chosen debt versus self-erasure, actual-error recovery and the unshown Final result. Do not invent these to complete a model.
2. **Hikaru/Yodaka post-V13 interval and motives:**ordinary life, guardianship/coaching, sustained autonomous conduct and hidden departure motives remain unshown; low-pressure interlocutors are sparse.
3. **Causal origins and negative constraints:**distinguish retrospective diagnosis from unique cause of Tsukasa’s eye/ethics and Inori’s capacity; broaden counterexample/transfer tests before any operational rule proposal.
4. **Visual geometry beyond the sample:**Japanese physical facing-page bindings/page turns and uninspected original sequences remain inherited descriptions. English packed spreads supply auxiliary matching, not universal equivalence.
5. **Language sampling:**more ordinary/institutional/stranger/sibling speech, humor/roleplay versus habitual register and actual regional forms with exceptions. No acoustic reconstruction from print.
6. **Scoped governance blocker:**separately authorize/review the exact fifteen-seal Medalist terminal-closure record and synchronized terminal front-matter, then validate CLOSED without weakening controls or admitting new sources; retain this no-main hold. The owner explicitly kept this publication series-only after the failed narrow trial.

### EC01 — Effort recognition before skill assessment

**Verdict/severity:** `SUPPORTED_WITH_BOUND` / LOW. Reader:early_sources.

**Stable authorities, baseline current versions:** MD-EV-V01-0007; MD-CL-0001@15; MD-CL-0002@15; MD-CL-0004@13. Active visual0012 dependencies use@16 after this correction.

**Original source locator(s):** MD-MG-JP-TB-V001-W01; Score 1; actually inspected original occurrence(s) 43; 44; 45; 46; 47; listed original pages; region described in source account / canonical locator | MD-EV-V01-0007 — Score 1, 0044–0053, affirmation, silent skating and edge/posture diagrams | “君は十分偉いよ” precedes skill assessment; helmet reminder; self-taught speed and individualized weight cue; child says 楽しい. | Trial; OBSERVATION plus attributed technical statements | HIGH visible change; speed unmeasured, physiology unverified. | 0002, 0004, 0012, 0013 |. The witness/member manifest below resolves exact resources and hashes; Score/component and panel region are retained here.

**Diagnostic wording/observation:** 君は十分偉いよ.

**Channel and knowledge:** tailed dialogue Tsukasa→Inori; subsequent coach interior reaction over blade/glide images Inori hears the effort affirmation before the trial. In image 0047 Tsukasa’s talent assessment addresses Hitomi, who responds and challenges his wording. The mother’s later permission is outside this worker’s inspected window. Reader access to his interior astonishment does not make it speech to Inori.

**Baseline formulation:** path: series/medalist/04 Character and Relationship Synthesis/inori-yuitsuka/MEDALIST_INORI_YUITSUKA_PROVISIONAL_MONOGRAPH.md; line: 80; wording: The plea follows an adult's affirmation that precedes any assessment of skill (`MD-EV-V01-0007`). Only then does she say why skating matters to her (`MD-EV-V01-0008`). Within the volume she chooses an early test, asks for help toward the world's best after meeting Hikaru Kamisaki, and refuses to guess what Tsukasa Akeuraji wants when choosing a training path (`MD-EV-V01-0010`, `MD-EV-V01-0018`, `MD-EV-V01-0025`). `MD-CL-0002@1` reads this as voiced agency expanding, with compliance toward an admired adult as a live alternative. Two things set here persist. The permission of her mother, Nozomi, runs only through middle school (`MD-EV-V01-0014`), and that term has never been revisited on the page. And concealment came first; the candour had to be invited.

**Independent factual account and finding:** Tsukasa tells Inori 君は十分偉いよ before watching the trial glide which prompts his marked surprise and then a spoken talent assessment to Hitomi. This is recognition of already expended effort rather than a reward for the demonstrated giftedness. The episode does not by itself make every later decision nonconditional.

**Strongest counterreading/uncertainty:** After watching the trial, Tsukasa adds a talent assessment in his exchange with Hitomi at image 0047. Effort recognition and talent-based reasons for instruction therefore coexist. This window does not show the mother’s later permission being granted.

**Dependencies/disposition/final verification:** MD-CS-0005; MD-RL-0001; Inori developmental argument; Tsukasa causal argument. Retain the before-assessment formulation; record original-page verification. Do not expand one scene into a universal claim about nonconditionality. Root accepted the correction or retained its stated bound after source-based comparison; all affected current formulations and historical erratum routes are represented in the correction register.

The same caution applies to the stairwell, where she again restates him correctly.; path: series/medalist/05 Specialist Synthesis/MEDALIST_INTEGRATED_PROVISIONAL_SYNTHESIS_THROUGH_V15.md; line: 46; wording: **The final, Volumes 14 and 15.** In Beijing Inori skates the same short program clean and is second by 0.33 (`MD-EV-V14-0033`, `MD-EV-V15-0009`). The fourteenth volume spends most of its pages teaching a spectator what the artistic mark measures. The fifteenth spends most of its pages off the ice. The trainer Kanayumi Miho tells Tsukasa that Inori believes she skates at the cost of his life and that his conduct, not his words, sustains the belief (`MD-EV-V15-0017`, `0021`). He tells his patron, Kago Kōichi, the childhood he had left out, and is told that a problem must not be met with a trial of good and evil (`MD-EV-V15-0034`, `0035`). In a stairwell Inori says it to him herself, and says that she wants the weight (`MD-EV-V15-0048`). He promises her something about his own life (`MD-EV-V15-0050`). Kanayumi's phrase is Kanayumi's. Inori's own words to Tsukasa, as that section lists them, are a word for being rewarded, said of everyone if she wins, and 役割, a role, for what she thought she had been given.

**Independent factual account and finding:** On the bench Tsukasa says coaching her is no substitute; she repeats the consequence and vows to become strongest and happiest. Later she affirms the coaching dream was true. Current relationship older/newer distinction is supported. Integrated each said what was withheld sinceV09 and she says it herself can suggest an unshown old-career confession. The noun is an exact lexical defect with little semantic shift. Accurate restatement proves reception, not durable release.

**Strongest counterreading/uncertainty:** Training, ice dance, teachers, skating pleasure, temperament and ongoing learning can contribute. ### LC08 — SP7 equality vow is a framed comic anecdote and intended practice

**Verdict/severity:** `SUPPORTED_WITH_BOUND` / LOW linguistic/source-layer enrichment. Reader:late_form.

**Baseline formulation:** path: series/medalist/03 Longitudinal Ledgers/MEDALIST_VISUAL_FORM_AND_PERFORMANCE_LEDGER.md; line: 414; wording: | MD-VF-0132 | MD-MOT-0011; EV-0035/0036/0037 | 0141–0148, 0149–0156 | A parody in which a rescue takes place under water among bubbles; a side chapter whose quarrel is lettered in English as a failure of communication and which ends by pulling back to its listening subjects | Uses the extras to stage a meeting the mainline withheld, in another world, and to frame a coach's origin as an anecdote | MD-CL-0012@14/0017@10 | HIGH; the parody is not continuity |; path: series/medalist/05 Specialist Synthesis/MEDALIST_MANGA_FORM_AND_PERFORMANCE_SPECIALIST.md; line: 205; wording: **Address.** In V02 Tsukasa's formal second-person pronoun is used twice with opposite force, to dignify Inori, a child, and to hold Yodaka, a superior, to account. In V03 Sonidori Rio's word for father carries a gloss meaning this person. In V04 Rio's change is measured by address, from a bare title to Tsukasa's full name and then a possessive. Tsukasa is polite to his pupils throughout and rough only alone. Tsukasa's V05 declaration joins his private first-person pronoun to the respectful second person he has always given Inori; Inori's V08 answer copies its construction and exchanges subject and object. Regional forms mark the fields. Shirone is given no gendered caption, and the reading uses none.; path: series/medalist/04 Character and Relationship Synthesis/tsukasa-akeuraji/MEDALIST_TSUKASA_AKEURAJI_PROVISIONAL_MONOGRAPH.md; line: 84; wording: **Money, help and debt.** In V01 he refuses Kago Kōichi's funding and resists returning to the household as a burden (`MD-CS-0016`, `MD-RL-0018`). In V07 he buys a harness for the club with his own money, and in V08 he is found working nights on cracked ribs to pay for it and is ashamed to be seen (`MD-CS-0121`, `MD-CS-0144`). In V15 the younger man will skate only on what he earns, calls asking his parents unfilial, refuses a reduced fee, and regrets his one request the moment it is declined (`MD-CS-0321`, `MD-CS-0323`). When help comes his thought is that it must be repaid with results. This is one pattern in four volumes, shown in conduct and not only in report. `MD-CL-0017@11` reads it as the obstacle under a second pattern: he asks freely for technical knowledge and has never asked about himself.

**Independent factual account and finding:** Young Tsukasa learns basics from Kōhei and is assigned Juna for steps. Juna offers no敬語/hierarchy then demands reverence. After two weeks both perform forced high spirits and insulting jokes. Tsukasa cries a future vow to teach anyone as an equal. Pullback ends with adult storytelling to Inori and the three adults nearby. A learning history and intended equality practice are supported, with comic and secondhand frame. It cannot certify equality always achieved. Polite morphology coexists with slang; respect and grammatical politeness need distinct description.

**Strongest counterreading/uncertainty:** Exaggeration may show incompatibility rather than universal origin of pedagogy. A conditional vow needs independent later conduct to establish fulfilment.

**Dependencies/disposition/final verification:** MD-VF-0132; Peer versus pupil speech; Method development causal limits. ### FC01 — Production origin of Tsukasa is design evidence, not his psychological cause

**Baseline formulation:** Tsukasa monograph section 11: Tsukasa was added after the editor asked what adults were doing; a statement of design that bears on adult responsibility and nothing in his psychology. Integrated L92 gives the same design history. Monograph L42 separately says his strengths and the burden have the same origin.

**Independent factual account and finding:** The creator-back-story special describes Inori and Yo as the initial center, Yo buying skates and supporting Inori, then an editorial objection that two children alone against harsh fate left the adults unexplained. Tsukasa was created as the supporting adult. This confirms the published production-history statement. It cannot itself establish one causal mechanism for every aspect of Tsukasa's care.

**Strongest counterreading/uncertainty:** The existing monograph explicitly prevents production origin from becoming psychology, and its stronger psychological thesis relies on manga elsewhere. Any narrowing of L42 requires those manga tests; the fanbook alone neither proves nor disproves it.

**Strongest counterreading/uncertainty:** Crowd support need not remove a structural standard or the child’s private premise. Current standings cannot establish a final winner or prove future freedom.

**Dependencies/disposition/final verification:** permission/cost thesis; adult consensus boundary; official versus provisional result. ### RC06 — Gori’s smile is deliberate care despite cost

**Verdict/severity:** `SUPPORTED_WITH_BOUND` / MEDIUM: doctrinal disagreement matters. Reader:root.

**Stable authorities, baseline current versions:** MD-EV-V13-0037; MD-CL-0011@16; MD-CL-0026@9; MD-CL-0028@5. Active visual0012 dependencies use@16 after this correction.

**Original source locator(s):** MD-MG-JP-TB-V013-W01; 53; actually inspected original occurrence(s) 138; 139; 140; Gori’s demonstration, tailed doctrine and child face-reading image. The witness/member manifest below resolves exact resources and hashes; Score/component and panel region are retained here.

**Diagnostic wording/observation:** 俺はお前から何も奪われていないよ.

**Channel and knowledge:** Gori’s spoken teaching and enacted smile, received by Tsukasa; embedded child-directed reassurance is a demonstration of intended practice. Tsukasa hears this adult doctrine. It does not prove that Iruka heard these exact words now, that the adult feels no pain, or that Inori has received it.

**Baseline formulation:** path: series/medalist/05 Specialist Synthesis/MEDALIST_INTEGRATED_PROVISIONAL_SYNTHESIS_THROUGH_V15.md; baseline_line: 64; wording: The adults' answer, when the work finally states it, is the same in five voices in one volume (`MD-CL-0026@9`). Takamine Shō, Tsukasa's teacher: those who support a skater are participants in the sport and not sacrifices to her; no one continues alone; be willing to burden someone, or stop (`MD-EV-V15-0026`). Meiko, Kago's wife: a problem shared may be solved at nobody's expense (`MD-EV-V15-0031`). Kanayumi: sufferings are not to be compared or made small (`MD-EV-V15-0023`). Kago: finding a cause is not sentencing a person, and taking all the blame is one way of not thinking (`MD-EV-V15-0034`; `MD-CL-0031@1`). A film, through Karasuba Daria's program: pain can be made known by making something of it, where otherwise a life would have to be spent (`MD-EV-V15-0008`).

**Independent factual account and finding:** The report of many losses precedes a deliberate smile. Gori says a child reads faces as reward or punishment and chooses an expression conveying that she has taken nothing from him. Costly work and refusal to burden the child coexist.

**Strongest counterreading/uncertainty:** This can be reassurance or performed emotional shielding; Kanayumi later challenges managing what Inori sees. The two adults’ methods cannot be collapsed into one universally approved technique.

**Dependencies/disposition/final verification:** adult convergence; face-reading mechanism; speech versus demonstration. Preserve Gori’s distinct proposition and compare it with later anti-acting advice. Narrow integrated five-voices wording to related answers, not unanimous coaching method. Root accepted the correction or retained its stated bound after source-based comparison; all affected current formulations and historical erratum routes are represented in the correction register.
